    'ERP_YYY_GXXQ' AS ERPAppCode,                              -- ERP平台编码
    1 AS BusinessOperationType,
    CONVERT(NVARCHAR(50), a.Id) AS ERPGoodsStockChangeRecordId,-- 主键Id
    a.[商品ID] AS ERPGoodsId,                                  -- 商品Id
    a.[商品名称] AS ERPGoodsName,                              -- ERP商品名称
    IIF(a.[批号] = '' OR a.[批号] IS NULL, '-', a.[批号]) AS BatchNumber, -- 批号
    a.[批次号] AS BatchNo,                                     -- 批次
    IIF(a.[变更数量] IS NULL, 0, a.[变更数量]) AS StockChangedCount, -- 变更数量（测试环境临时把 null 处理成 0）
    a.[变更时间] AS StockChangedCountTime,                     -- 变更时间
    a.[业务类型] AS BillType,                                  -- 业务单据类型
    a.[业务单据名称] AS BillTypeName,                          -- 业务单据名称
    a.[业务单据号] AS BillNo,                                  -- 业务单据号
    a.[货主Id] AS ERPHZId,                                     -- 货主Id（货主编号）
    a.[销售订单号] AS ERPOrderId,                              -- ERP销售订单号
    -- 效期字段标准化处理：支持多种格式（YYYY-MM, YYYYMM, YYYYMMDD, YYYY-MM-DD）
    CONVERT(NVARCHAR(50),
        CASE
            WHEN LEN(a.[效期]) = 7 AND ISDATE(a.[效期] + '-01') = 1 THEN
                DATEADD(DAY, -1, DATEADD(MONTH, 1, CONVERT(DATETIME2, a.[效期] + '-01')))
            WHEN LEN(a.[效期]) = 10 AND ISDATE(a.[效期]) = 1 THEN
                CONVERT(DATETIME2, a.[效期])
            WHEN LEN(a.[效期]) = 6 AND ISDATE(LEFT(a.[效期], 4) + '-' + RIGHT(a.[效期], 2) + '-01') = 1 THEN
                CONVERT(DATETIME2, LEFT(a.[效期], 4) + '-' + RIGHT(a.[效期], 2) + '-01')
            WHEN LEN(a.[效期]) = 8 AND ISDATE(LEFT(a.[效期], 4) + '-' + SUBSTRING(a.[效期], 5, 2) + '-' + RIGHT(a.[效期], 2)) = 1 THEN
                CONVERT(DATETIME2, LEFT(a.[效期], 4) + '-' + SUBSTRING(a.[效期], 5, 2) + '-' + RIGHT(a.[效期], 2))
            ELSE
                CONVERT(DATETIME2, '9999-12-31')
        END,
        23
    ) AS ValidityPeriod, -- 效期
    -- 生产日期字段标准化处理（逻辑同效期）
    CONVERT(NVARCHAR(50),
        CASE
            WHEN LEN(a.[生产日期]) = 7 AND ISDATE(a.[生产日期] + '-01') = 1 THEN
                DATEADD(DAY, -1, DATEADD(MONTH, 1, CONVERT(DATETIME2, a.[生产日期] + '-01')))
            WHEN LEN(a.[生产日期]) = 10 AND ISDATE(a.[生产日期]) = 1 THEN
                CONVERT(DATETIME2, a.[生产日期])
            WHEN LEN(a.[生产日期]) = 6 AND ISDATE(LEFT(a.[生产日期], 4) + '-' + RIGHT(a.[生产日期], 2) + '-01') = 1 THEN
                CONVERT(DATETIME2, LEFT(a.[生产日期], 4) + '-' + RIGHT(a.[生产日期], 2) + '-01')
            WHEN LEN(a.[生产日期]) = 8 AND ISDATE(LEFT(a.[生产日期], 4) + '-' + SUBSTRING(a.[生产日期], 5, 2) + '-' + RIGHT(a.[生产日期], 2)) = 1 THEN
                CONVERT(DATETIME2, LEFT(a.[生产日期], 4) + '-' + SUBSTRING(a.[生产日期], 5, 2) + '-' + RIGHT(a.[生产日期], 2))
            ELSE
                CONVERT(DATETIME2, '9999-12-31')
        END,
        23
    ) AS ManufactureDate, -- 生产日期
    IIF(a.[件装量] IS NULL, 1, a.[件装量]) AS PackingCapacity, -- 件装量
    b.StockType AS StockType,                                 -- 库存类型
    a.[行号] AS RowIndex,                                     -- 行号
    'ERP_YYY_GXXQ' AS WarehouseCode,                          -- 仓库编号
    -- ERP商品库存Id：货主ID_商品ID_批号_批次号_库存类型
    a.[货主ID] + '_' + a.[商品ID] + '_' + IIF(a.[批号] = '' OR a.[批号] IS NULL, '-', a.[批号]) + '_' + a.[批次号] + '_' + CONVERT(NVARCHAR(10), b.StockType) AS ERPGoodsStockId,
    -- 业务订单号：优先使用 [业务订单号]，为空则用 [业务单据号]
    IIF(a.[业务订单号] IS NULL OR a.[业务订单号] = '', a.[业务单据号], a.[业务订单号]) AS BusinessOrderNo -- 业务订单号
FROM
    [dbo].[ywlsb_center_v] AS a
    INNER JOIN [dbo].[ckhzdzb] AS b ON a.[仓库ID] = b.ckid AND a.[货主Id] = b.hzid

    a.[业务单据号] AS BillNo,                                  -- 业务单据号
    a.[货主Id] AS ERPHZId,                                     -- 货主Id（货主编号）
    a.[销售订单号] AS ERPOrderId,                              -- ERP销售订单号
    -- 效期字段标准化处理：支持多种格式（YYYY-MM, YYYYMM, YYYYMMDD, YYYY-MM-DD）
    CONVERT(NVARCHAR(50),
        CASE
            WHEN LEN(a.[效期]) = 7 AND ISDATE(a.[效期] + '-01') = 1 THEN
                DATEADD(DAY, -1, DATEADD(MONTH, 1, CONVERT(DATETIME2, a.[效期] + '-01')))
            WHEN LEN(a.[效期]) = 10 AND ISDATE(a.[效期]) = 1 THEN
                CONVERT(DATETIME2, a.[效期])
            WHEN LEN(a.[效期]) = 6 AND ISDATE(LEFT(a.[效期], 4) + '-' + RIGHT(a.[效期], 2) + '-01') = 1 THEN
                CONVERT(DATETIME2, LEFT(a.[效期], 4) + '-' + RIGHT(a.[效期], 2) + '-01')
            WHEN LEN(a.[效期]) = 8 AND ISDATE(LEFT(a.[效期], 4) + '-' + SUBSTRING(a.[效期], 5, 2) + '-' + RIGHT(a.[效期], 2)) = 1 THEN
                CONVERT(DATETIME2, LEFT(a.[效期], 4) + '-' + SUBSTRING(a.[效期], 5, 2) + '-' + RIGHT(a.[效期], 2))
            ELSE
                CONVERT(DATETIME2, '9999-12-31')
        END,
        23
    ) AS ValidityPeriod, -- 效期
    -- 生产日期字段标准化处理（逻辑同效期）
    CONVERT(NVARCHAR(50),
        CASE
            WHEN LEN(a.[生产日期]) = 7 AND ISDATE(a.[生产日期] + '-01') = 1 THEN
                DATEADD(DAY, -1, DATEADD(MONTH, 1, CONVERT(DATETIME2, a.[生产日期] + '-01')))
            WHEN LEN(a.[生产日期]) = 10 AND ISDATE(a.[生产日期]) = 1 THEN
                CONVERT(DATETIME2, a.[生产日期])
            WHEN LEN(a.[生产日期]) = 6 AND ISDATE(LEFT(a.[生产日期], 4) + '-' + RIGHT(a.[生产日期], 2) + '-01') = 1 THEN
                CONVERT(DATETIME2, LEFT(a.[生产日期], 4) + '-' + RIGHT(a.[生产日期], 2) + '-01')
            WHEN LEN(a.[生产日期]) = 8 AND ISDATE(LEFT(a.[生产日期], 4) + '-' + SUBSTRING(a.[生产日期], 5, 2) + '-' + RIGHT(a.[生产日期], 2)) = 1 THEN
                CONVERT(DATETIME2, LEFT(a.[生产日期], 4) + '-' + SUBSTRING(a.[生产日期], 5, 2) + '-' + RIGHT(a.[生产日期], 2))
            ELSE
                CONVERT(DATETIME2, '9999-12-31')
        END,
        23
    ) AS ManufactureDate, -- 生产日期
    IIF(a.[件装量] IS NULL, 1, a.[件装量]) AS PackingCapacity, -- 件装量
    b.StockType AS StockType,                                 -- 库存类型
    a.[行号] AS RowIndex,                                     -- 行号
    'ERP_YYY_GXXQ' AS WarehouseCode,                          -- 仓库编号
    -- ERP商品库存Id：货主ID_商品ID_批号_批次号_库存类型
    a.[货主ID] + '_' + a.[商品ID] + '_' + IIF(a.[批号] = '' OR a.[批号] IS NULL, '-', a.[批号]) + '_' + a.[批次号] + '_' + CONVERT(NVARCHAR(10), b.StockType) AS ERPGoodsStockId,
    -- 业务订单号：优先使用 [业务订单号]，为空则用 [业务单据号]
    IIF(a.[业务订单号] IS NULL OR a.[业务订单号] = '', a.[业务单据号], a.[业务订单号]) AS BusinessOrderNo -- 业务订单号
FROM
    [dbo].[ywlsb_center_v] AS a

    IIF(a.[变更数量] IS NULL, 0, a.[变更数量]) AS StockChangedCount, -- 变更数量（测试环境临时把 null 处理成 0）
    a.[变更时间] AS StockChangedCountTime,                     -- 变更时间
    a.[业务类型] AS BillType,                                  -- 业务单据类型
    a.[业务单据名称] AS BillTypeName,                          -- 业务单据名称
    a.[业务单据号] AS BillNo,                                  -- 业务单据号
    a.[货主Id] AS ERPHZId,                                     -- 货主Id（货主编号）
    a.[销售订单号] AS ERPOrderId,                              -- ERP销售订单号
    -- 效期字段标准化处理：支持多种格式（YYYY-MM, YYYYMM, YYYYMMDD, YYYY-MM-DD）
    CONVERT(NVARCHAR(50),
        CASE
            WHEN LEN(a.[效期]) = 7 AND ISDATE(a.[效期] + '-01') = 1 THEN
                DATEADD(DAY, -1, DATEADD(MONTH, 1, CONVERT(DATETIME2, a.[效期] + '-01')))
            WHEN LEN(a.[效期]) = 10 AND ISDATE(a.[效期]) = 1 THEN
                CONVERT(DATETIME2, a.[效期])
            WHEN LEN(a.[效期]) = 6 AND ISDATE(LEFT(a.[效期], 4) + '-' + RIGHT(a.[效期], 2) + '-01') = 1 THEN
                CONVERT(DATETIME2, LEFT(a.[效期], 4) + '-' + RIGHT(a.[效期], 2) + '-01')
            WHEN LEN(a.[效期]) = 8 AND ISDATE(LEFT(a.[效期], 4) + '-' + SUBSTRING(a.[效期], 5, 2) + '-' + RIGHT(a.[效期], 2)) = 1 THEN
                CONVERT(DATETIME2, LEFT(a.[效期], 4) + '-' + SUBSTRING(a.[效期], 5, 2) + '-' + RIGHT(a.[效期], 2))
            ELSE
                CONVERT(DATETIME2, '9999-12-31')
        END,
        23
    ) AS ValidityPeriod, -- 效期
    -- 生产日期字段标准化处理（逻辑同效期）
    CONVERT(NVARCHAR(50),
        CASE
            WHEN LEN(a.[生产日期]) = 7 AND ISDATE(a.[生产日期] + '-01') = 1 THEN
                DATEADD(DAY, -1, DATEADD(MONTH, 1, CONVERT(DATETIME2, a.[生产日期] + '-01')))
            WHEN LEN(a.[生产日期]) = 10 AND ISDATE(a.[生产日期]) = 1 THEN
                CONVERT(DATETIME2, a.[生产日期])
            WHEN LEN(a.[生产日期]) = 6 AND ISDATE(LEFT(a.[生产日期], 4) + '-' + RIGHT(a.[生产日期], 2) + '-01') = 1 THEN
                CONVERT(DATETIME2, LEFT(a.[生产日期], 4) + '-' + RIGHT(a.[生产日期], 2) + '-01')
            WHEN LEN(a.[生产日期]) = 8 AND ISDATE(LEFT(a.[生产日期], 4) + '-' + SUBSTRING(a.[生产日期], 5, 2) + '-' + RIGHT(a.[生产日期], 2)) = 1 THEN
                CONVERT(DATETIME2, LEFT(a.[生产日期], 4) + '-' + SUBSTRING(a.[生产日期], 5, 2) + '-' + RIGHT(a.[生产日期], 2))
            ELSE
                CONVERT(DATETIME2, '9999-12-31')
        END,
        23
    ) AS ManufactureDate, -- 生产日期
    IIF(a.[件装量] IS NULL, 1, a.[件装量]) AS PackingCapacity, -- 件装量
    b.StockType AS StockType,                                 -- 库存类型
    a.[行号] AS RowIndex,                                     -- 行号
    'ERP_YYY_GXXQ' AS WarehouseCode,                          -- 仓库编号
    -- ERP商品库存Id：货主ID_商品ID_批号_批次号_库存类型
    a.[货主ID] + '_' + a.[商品ID] + '_' + IIF(a.[批号] = '' OR a.[批号] IS NULL, '-', a.[批号]) + '_' + a.[批次号] + '_' + CONVERT(NVARCHAR(10), b.StockType) AS ERPGoodsStockId,
    -- 业务订单号：优先使用 [业务订单号]，为空则用 [业务单据号]
    IIF(a.[业务订单号] IS NULL OR a.[业务订单号] = '', a.[业务单据号], a.[业务订单号]) AS BusinessOrderNo -- 业务订单号
FROM
    [dbo].[ywlsb_center_v] AS a
    INNER JOIN [dbo].[ckhzdzb] AS b ON a.[仓库ID] = b.ckid AND a.[货主Id] = b.hzid

    a.[货主ID] + '_' + a.[商品ID] + '_' + IIF(a.[批号] = '' OR a.[批号] IS NULL, '-', a.[批号]) + '_' + a.[批次号] + '_' + CONVERT(NVARCHAR(10), b.StockType) AS ERPGoodsStockId,
    -- 业务订单号：优先使用 [业务订单号]，为空则用 [业务单据号]
    IIF(a.[业务订单号] IS NULL OR a.[业务订单号] = '', a.[业务单据号], a.[业务订单号]) AS BusinessOrderNo -- 业务订单号
FROM
    [dbo].[ywlsb_center_v] AS a
    INNER JOIN [dbo].[ckhzdzb] AS b ON a.[仓库ID] = b.ckid AND a.[货主Id] = b.hzid

 -- 效期字段标准化处理：支持多种格式（YYYY-MM, YYYYMM, YYYYMMDD, YYYY-MM-DD）
 CONVERT(NVARCHAR(50),
 CASE
 WHEN LEN(a.[效期]) = 7 AND ISDATE(a.[效期] + '-01') = 1 THEN
 DATEADD(DAY, -1, DATEADD(MONTH, 1, CONVERT(DATETIME2, a.[效期] + '-01')))
 WHEN LEN(a.[效期]) = 10 AND ISDATE(a.[效期]) = 1 THEN
 CONVERT(DATETIME2, a.[效期])
 WHEN LEN(a.[效期]) = 6 AND ISDATE(LEFT(a.[效期], 4) + '-' + RIGHT(a.[效期], 2) + '-01') = 1 THEN
 CONVERT(DATETIME2, LEFT(a.[效期], 4) + '-' + RIGHT(a.[效期], 2) + '-01')
 WHEN LEN(a.[效期]) = 8 AND ISDATE(LEFT(a.[效期], 4) + '-' + SUBSTRING(a.[效期], 5, 2) + '-' + RIGHT(a.[效期], 2)) = 1 THEN
 CONVERT(DATETIME2, LEFT(a.[效期], 4) + '-' + SUBSTRING(a.[效期], 5, 2) + '-' + RIGHT(a.[效期], 2))
 ELSE
 CONVERT(DATETIME2, '9999-12-31')
 END,
 23
 ) AS ValidityPeriod, -- 效期
 -- 生产日期字段标准化处理（逻辑同效期）
 CONVERT(NVARCHAR(50),
 CASE
 WHEN LEN(a.[生产日期]) = 7 AND ISDATE(a.[生产日期] + '-01') = 1 THEN
 DATEADD(DAY, -1, DATEADD(MONTH, 1, CONVERT(DATETIME2, a.[生产日期] + '-01')))
 WHEN LEN(a.[生产日期]) = 10 AND ISDATE(a.[生产日期]) = 1 THEN
 CONVERT(DATETIME2, a.[生产日期])
 WHEN LEN(a.[生产日期]) = 6 AND ISDATE(LEFT(a.[生产日期], 4) + '-' + RIGHT(a.[生产日期], 2) + '-01') = 1 THEN
 CONVERT(DATETIME2, LEFT(a.[生产日期], 4) + '-' + RIGHT(a.[生产日期], 2) + '-01')
 WHEN LEN(a.[生产日期]) = 8 AND ISDATE(LEFT(a.[生产日期], 4) + '-' + SUBSTRING(a.[生产日期], 5, 2) + '-' + RIGHT(a.[生产日期], 2)) = 1 THEN
 CONVERT(DATETIME2, LEFT(a.[生产日期], 4) + '-' + SUBSTRING(a.[生产日期], 5, 2) + '-' + RIGHT(a.[生产日期], 2))
 ELSE
 CONVERT(DATETIME2, '9999-12-31')
 END,
 23
 ) AS ManufactureDate, -- 生产日期
 IIF(a.[件装量] IS NULL, 1, a.[件装量]) AS PackingCapacity, -- 件装量
 b.StockType AS StockType, -- 库存类型
 a.[行号] AS RowIndex, -- 行号
 'ERP_YYY_GXXQ' AS WarehouseCode, -- 仓库编号
 -- ERP商品库存Id：货主ID_商品ID_批号_批次号_库存类型
 a.[货主ID] + '_' + a.[商品ID] + '_' + IIF(a.[批号] = '' OR a.[批号] IS NULL, '-', a.[批号]) + '_' + a.[批次号] + '_' + CONVERT(NVARCHAR(10), b.StockType) AS ERPGoodsStockId,
 -- 业务订单号：优先使用 [业务订单号]，为空则用 [业务单据号]
 IIF(a.[业务订单号] IS NULL OR a.[业务订单号] = '', a.[业务单据号], a.[业务订单号]) AS BusinessOrderNo -- 业务订单号
FROM
 [dbo].[ywlsb_center_v] AS a
 INNER JOIN [dbo].[ckhzdzb] AS b ON a.[仓库ID] = b.ckid AND a.[货主Id] = b.hzid

 CONVERT(DATETIME2, LEFT(a.[生产日期], 4) + '-' + SUBSTRING(a.[生产日期], 5, 2) + '-' + RIGHT(a.[生产日期], 2))
 ELSE
 CONVERT(DATETIME2, '9999-12-31')
 END,

 ) AS ManufactureDate, -- 生产日期

 b.StockType AS StockType, -- 库存类型
 a.[行号] AS RowIndex, -- 行号

2026-02-04 19:31:36.730 +08:00 [INF] SQL after removing disabled conditions:
SELECT top 1000
 1 AS BusinessOperationType,
 CONVERT(NVARCHAR(50), a.Id) AS ERPGoodsStockChangeRecordId,-- 主键Id
 a.[商品ID] AS ERPGoodsId, -- 商品Id
 a.[商品名称] AS ERPGoodsName, -- ERP商品名称
 IIF(a.[批号] = '' OR a.[批号] IS NULL, '-', a.[批号]) AS BatchNumber, -- 批号
 a.[批次号] AS BatchNo, -- 批次
 IIF(a.[变更数量] IS NULL, 0, a.[变更数量]) AS StockChangedCount, -- 变更数量（测试环境临时把 null 处理成 0）
 a.[变更时间] AS StockChangedCountTime, -- 变更时间
 a.[业务类型] AS BillType, -- 业务单据类型
 a.[业务单据名称] AS BillTypeName, -- 业务单据名称
 a.[业务单据号] AS BillNo, -- 业务单据号
 a.[货主Id] AS ERPHZId, -- 货主Id（货主编号）
 a.[销售订单号] AS ERPOrderId, -- ERP销售订单号
 -- 效期字段标准化处理：支持多种格式（YYYY-MM, YYYYMM, YYYYMMDD, YYYY-MM-DD）
 CONVERT(NVARCHAR(50),
 CASE
 WHEN LEN(a.[效期]) = 7 AND ISDATE(a.[效期] + '-01') = 1 THEN
 DATEADD(DAY, -1, DATEADD(MONTH, 1, CONVERT(DATETIME2, a.[效期] + '-01')))
 WHEN LEN(a.[效期]) = 10 AND ISDATE(a.[效期]) = 1 THEN
 CONVERT(DATETIME2, a.[效期])
 WHEN LEN(a.[效期]) = 6 AND ISDATE(LEFT(a.[效期], 4) + '-' + RIGHT(a.[效期], 2) + '-01') = 1 THEN
 CONVERT(DATETIME2, LEFT(a.[效期], 4) + '-' + RIGHT(a.[效期], 2) + '-01')
 WHEN LEN(a.[效期]) = 8 AND ISDATE(LEFT(a.[效期], 4) + '-' + SUBSTRING(a.[效期], 5, 2) + '-' + RIGHT(a.[效期], 2)) = 1 THEN
 CONVERT(DATETIME2, LEFT(a.[效期], 4) + '-' + SUBSTRING(a.[效期], 5, 2) + '-' + RIGHT(a.[效期], 2))
 ELSE
 CONVERT(DATETIME2, '9999-12-31')
 END,
 23
 ) AS ValidityPeriod, -- 效期
 -- 生产日期字段标准化处理（逻辑同效期）
 CONVERT(NVARCHAR(50),
 CASE
 WHEN LEN(a.[生产日期]) = 7 AND ISDATE(a.[生产日期] + '-01') = 1 THEN
 DATEADD(DAY, -1, DATEADD(MONTH, 1, CONVERT(DATETIME2, a.[生产日期] + '-01')))
 WHEN LEN(a.[生产日期]) = 10 AND ISDATE(a.[生产日期]) = 1 THEN
 CONVERT(DATETIME2, a.[生产日期])
 WHEN LEN(a.[生产日期]) = 6 AND ISDATE(LEFT(a.[生产日期], 4) + '-' + RIGHT(a.[生产日期], 2) + '-01') = 1 THEN
 CONVERT(DATETIME2, LEFT(a.[生产日期], 4) + '-' + RIGHT(a.[生产日期], 2) + '-01')
 WHEN LEN(a.[生产日期]) = 8 AND ISDATE(LEFT(a.[生产日期], 4) + '-' + SUBSTRING(a.[生产日期], 5, 2) + '-' + RIGHT(a.[生产日期], 2)) = 1 THEN
 CONVERT(DATETIME2, LEFT(a.[生产日期], 4) + '-' + SUBSTRING(a.[生产日期], 5, 2) + '-' + RIGHT(a.[生产日期], 2))
 ELSE
 CONVERT(DATETIME2, '9999-12-31')
 END,
 23
 ) AS ManufactureDate, -- 生产日期
 IIF(a.[件装量] IS NULL, 1, a.[件装量]) AS PackingCapacity, -- 件装量
 b.StockType AS StockType, -- 库存类型
 a.[行号] AS RowIndex, -- 行号
 'ERP_YYY_GXXQ' AS WarehouseCode, -- 仓库编号
 -- ERP商品库存Id：货主ID_商品ID_批号_批次号_库存类型
 a.[货主ID] + '_' + a.[商品ID] + '_' + IIF(a.[批号] = '' OR a.[批号] IS NULL, '-', a.[批号]) + '_' + a.[批次号] + '_' + CONVERT(NVARCHAR(10), b.StockType) AS ERPGoodsStockId,
 -- 业务订单号：优先使用 [业务订单号]，为空则用 [业务单据号]
 IIF(a.[业务订单号] IS NULL OR a.[业务订单号] = '', a.[业务单据号], a.[业务订单号]) AS BusinessOrderNo -- 业务订单号
FROM
 [dbo].[ywlsb_center_v] AS a
 INNER JOIN [dbo].[ckhzdzb] AS b ON a.[仓库ID] = b.ckid AND a.[货主Id] = b.hzid

 CONVERT(DATETIME2, a.[生产日期])
 WHEN LEN(a.[生产日期]) = 6 AND ISDATE(LEFT(a.[生产日期], 4) + '-' + RIGHT(a.[生产日期], 2) + '-01') = 1 THEN
 CONVERT(DATETIME2, LEFT(a.[生产日期], 4) + '-' + RIGHT(a.[生产日期], 2) + '-01')
 WHEN LEN(a.[生产日期]) = 8 AND ISDATE(LEFT(a.[生产日期], 4) + '-' + SUBSTRING(a.[生产日期], 5, 2) + '-' + RIGHT(a.[生产日期], 2)) = 1 THEN
 CONVERT(DATETIME2, LEFT(a.[生产日期], 4) + '-' + SUBSTRING(a.[生产日期], 5, 2) + '-' + RIGHT(a.[生产日期], 2))
 ELSE
 CONVERT(DATETIME2, '9999-12-31')
 END,
 23
 ) AS ManufactureDate, -- 生产日期
 IIF(a.[件装量] IS NULL, 1, a.[件装量]) AS PackingCapacity, -- 件装量
 b.StockType AS StockType, -- 库存类型
 a.[行号] AS RowIndex, -- 行号
 'ERP_YYY_GXXQ' AS WarehouseCode, -- 仓库编号
 -- ERP商品库存Id：货主ID_商品ID_批号_批次号_库存类型
 a.[货主ID] + '_' + a.[商品ID] + '_' + IIF(a.[批号] = '' OR a.[批号] IS NULL, '-', a.[批号]) + '_' + a.[批次号] + '_' + CONVERT(NVARCHAR(10), b.StockType) AS ERPGoodsStockId,
 -- 业务订单号：优先使用 [业务订单号]，为空则用 [业务单据号]
 IIF(a.[业务订单号] IS NULL OR a.[业务订单号] = '', a.[业务单据号], a.[业务订单号]) AS BusinessOrderNo -- 业务订单号
FROM
 [dbo].[ywlsb_center_v] AS a
 INNER JOIN [dbo].[ckhzdzb] AS b ON a.[仓库ID] = b.ckid AND a.[货主Id] = b.hzid

 DATEADD(DAY, -1, DATEADD(MONTH, 1, CONVERT(DATETIME2, a.[效期] + '-01')))
 WHEN LEN(a.[效期]) = 10 AND ISDATE(a.[效期]) = 1 THEN
 CONVERT(DATETIME2, a.[效期])
 WHEN LEN(a.[效期]) = 6 AND ISDATE(LEFT(a.[效期], 4) + '-' + RIGHT(a.[效期], 2) + '-01') = 1 THEN
 CONVERT(DATETIME2, LEFT(a.[效期], 4) + '-' + RIGHT(a.[效期], 2) + '-01')
 WHEN LEN(a.[效期]) = 8 AND ISDATE(LEFT(a.[效期], 4) + '-' + SUBSTRING(a.[效期], 5, 2) + '-' + RIGHT(a.[效期], 2)) = 1 THEN
 CONVERT(DATETIME2, LEFT(a.[效期], 4) + '-' + SUBSTRING(a.[效期], 5, 2) + '-' + RIGHT(a.[效期], 2))
 ELSE
 CONVERT(DATETIME2, '9999-12-31')
 END,
 23
 ) AS ValidityPeriod, -- 效期

 CONVERT(NVARCHAR(50),
 CASE
 WHEN LEN(a.[生产日期]) = 7 AND ISDATE(a.[生产日期] + '-01') = 1 THEN
 DATEADD(DAY, -1, DATEADD(MONTH, 1, CONVERT(DATETIME2, a.[生产日期] + '-01')))

 a.[变更时间] AS StockChangedCountTime, -- 变更时间

 -- 效期字段标准化处理：支持多种格式（YYYY-MM, YYYYMM, YYYYMMDD, YYYY-MM-DD）
 CONVERT(NVARCHAR(50),
 CASE
 WHEN LEN(a.[效期]) = 7 AND ISDATE(a.[效期] + '-01') = 1 THEN
 DATEADD(DAY, -1, DATEADD(MONTH, 1, CONVERT(DATETIME2, a.[效期] + '-01')))
 WHEN LEN(a.[效期]) = 10 AND ISDATE(a.[效期]) = 1 THEN
 CONVERT(DATETIME2, a.[效期])
 WHEN LEN(a.[效期]) = 6 AND ISDATE(LEFT(a.[效期], 4) + '-' + RIGHT(a.[效期], 2) + '-01') = 1 THEN
 CONVERT(DATETIME2, LEFT(a.[效期], 4) + '-' + RIGHT(a.[效期], 2) + '-01')
 WHEN LEN(a.[效期]) = 8 AND ISDATE(LEFT(a.[效期], 4) + '-' + SUBSTRING(a.[效期], 5, 2) + '-' + RIGHT(a.[效期], 2)) = 1 THEN
 CONVERT(DATETIME2, LEFT(a.[效期], 4) + '-' + SUBSTRING(a.[效期], 5, 2) + '-' + RIGHT(a.[效期], 2))
 ELSE
 CONVERT(DATETIME2, '9999-12-31')
 END,
 23
 ) AS ValidityPeriod, -- 效期
 -- 生产日期字段标准化处理（逻辑同效期）
 CONVERT(NVARCHAR(50),
 CASE
 WHEN LEN(a.[生产日期]) = 7 AND ISDATE(a.[生产日期] + '-01') = 1 THEN
 DATEADD(DAY, -1, DATEADD(MONTH, 1, CONVERT(DATETIME2, a.[生产日期] + '-01')))
 WHEN LEN(a.[生产日期]) = 10 AND ISDATE(a.[生产日期]) = 1 THEN
 CONVERT(DATETIME2, a.[生产日期])

 CONVERT(DATETIME2, LEFT(a.[生产日期], 4) + '-' + SUBSTRING(a.[生产日期], 5, 2) + '-' + RIGHT(a.[生产日期], 2))

 -- ERP商品库存Id：货主ID_商品ID_批号_批次号_库存类型
 a.[货主ID] + '_' + a.[商品ID] + '_' + IIF(a.[批号] = '' OR a.[批号] IS NULL, '-', a.[批号]) + '_' + a.[批次号] + '_' + CONVERT(NVARCHAR(10), b.StockType) AS ERPGoodsStockId,
 -- 业务订单号：优先使用 [业务订单号]，为空则用 [业务单据号]

 ELSE
 CONVERT(DATETIME2, '9999-12-31')
 END,

SELECT top 1000
 1 AS BusinessOperationType,
 CONVERT(NVARCHAR(50), a.Id) AS ERPGoodsStockChangeRecordId,-- 主键Id
 a.[商品ID] AS ERPGoodsId, -- 商品Id
 a.[商品名称] AS ERPGoodsName, -- ERP商品名称
 IIF(a.[批号] = '' OR a.[批号] IS NULL, '-', a.[批号]) AS BatchNumber, -- 批号
 a.[批次号] AS BatchNo, -- 批次
 IIF(a.[变更数量] IS NULL, 0, a.[变更数量]) AS StockChangedCount, -- 变更数量（测试环境临时把 null 处理成 0）
 a.[变更时间] AS StockChangedCountTime, -- 变更时间
 a.[业务类型] AS BillType, -- 业务单据类型
 a.[业务单据名称] AS BillTypeName, -- 业务单据名称
 a.[业务单据号] AS BillNo, -- 业务单据号
 a.[货主Id] AS ERPHZId, -- 货主Id（货主编号）
 a.[销售订单号] AS ERPOrderId, -- ERP销售订单号
 -- 效期字段标准化处理：支持多种格式（YYYY-MM, YYYYMM, YYYYMMDD, YYYY-MM-DD）
 CONVERT(NVARCHAR(50),
 CASE
 WHEN LEN(a.[效期]) = 7 AND ISDATE(a.[效期] + '-01') = 1 THEN
 DATEADD(DAY, -1, DATEADD(MONTH, 1, CONVERT(DATETIME2, a.[效期] + '-01')))
 WHEN LEN(a.[效期]) = 10 AND ISDATE(a.[效期]) = 1 THEN
 CONVERT(DATETIME2, a.[效期])
 WHEN LEN(a.[效期]) = 6 AND ISDATE(LEFT(a.[效期], 4) + '-' + RIGHT(a.[效期], 2) + '-01') = 1 THEN
 CONVERT(DATETIME2, LEFT(a.[效期], 4) + '-' + RIGHT(a.[效期], 2) + '-01')
 WHEN LEN(a.[效期]) = 8 AND ISDATE(LEFT(a.[效期], 4) + '-' + SUBSTRING(a.[效期], 5, 2) + '-' + RIGHT(a.[效期], 2)) = 1 THEN
 CONVERT(DATETIME2, LEFT(a.[效期], 4) + '-' + SUBSTRING(a.[效期], 5, 2) + '-' + RIGHT(a.[效期], 2))
 ELSE
 CONVERT(DATETIME2, '9999-12-31')
 END,
 23
 ) AS ValidityPeriod, -- 效期
 -- 生产日期字段标准化处理（逻辑同效期）
 CONVERT(NVARCHAR(50),
 CASE
 WHEN LEN(a.[生产日期]) = 7 AND ISDATE(a.[生产日期] + '-01') = 1 THEN
 DATEADD(DAY, -1, DATEADD(MONTH, 1, CONVERT(DATETIME2, a.[生产日期] + '-01')))
 WHEN LEN(a.[生产日期]) = 10 AND ISDATE(a.[生产日期]) = 1 THEN
 CONVERT(DATETIME2, a.[生产日期])
 WHEN LEN(a.[生产日期]) = 6 AND ISDATE(LEFT(a.[生产日期], 4) + '-' + RIGHT(a.[生产日期], 2) + '-01') = 1 THEN
 CONVERT(DATETIME2, LEFT(a.[生产日期], 4) + '-' + RIGHT(a.[生产日期], 2) + '-01')
 WHEN LEN(a.[生产日期]) = 8 AND ISDATE(LEFT(a.[生产日期], 4) + '-' + SUBSTRING(a.[生产日期], 5, 2) + '-' + RIGHT(a.[生产日期], 2)) = 1 THEN
 CONVERT(DATETIME2, LEFT(a.[生产日期], 4) + '-' + SUBSTRING(a.[生产日期], 5, 2) + '-' + RIGHT(a.[生产日期], 2))
 ELSE
 CONVERT(DATETIME2, '9999-12-31')
 END,
 23
 ) AS ManufactureDate, -- 生产日期
 IIF(a.[件装量] IS NULL, 1, a.[件装量]) AS PackingCapacity, -- 件装量
 b.StockType AS StockType, -- 库存类型
 a.[行号] AS RowIndex, -- 行号
 'ERP_YYY_GXXQ' AS WarehouseCode, -- 仓库编号
 -- ERP商品库存Id：货主ID_商品ID_批号_批次号_库存类型
 a.[货主ID] + '_' + a.[商品ID] + '_' + IIF(a.[批号] = '' OR a.[批号] IS NULL, '-', a.[批号]) + '_' + a.[批次号] + '_' + CONVERT(NVARCHAR(10), b.StockType) AS ERPGoodsStockId,
 -- 业务订单号：优先使用 [业务订单号]，为空则用 [业务单据号]
 IIF(a.[业务订单号] IS NULL OR a.[业务订单号] = '', a.[业务单据号], a.[业务订单号]) AS BusinessOrderNo -- 业务订单号
FROM
 [dbo].[ywlsb_center_v] AS a
 INNER JOIN [dbo].[ckhzdzb] AS b ON a.[仓库ID] = b.ckid AND a.[货主Id] = b.hzid
WHERE 1=1 and a.[商品ID]=@ERPGoodsId and a.[业务订单号]=@BusinessOrderNo

 IIF(a.[批号] = '' OR a.[批号] IS NULL, '-', a.[批号]) AS BatchNumber, -- 批号
 a.[批次号] AS BatchNo, -- 批次
 IIF(a.[变更数量] IS NULL, 0, a.[变更数量]) AS StockChangedCount, -- 变更数量（测试环境临时把 null 处理成 0）
 a.[变更时间] AS StockChangedCountTime, -- 变更时间
 a.[业务类型] AS BillType, -- 业务单据类型
 a.[业务单据名称] AS BillTypeName, -- 业务单据名称
 a.[业务单据号] AS BillNo, -- 业务单据号
 a.[货主Id] AS ERPHZId, -- 货主Id（货主编号）
 a.[销售订单号] AS ERPOrderId, -- ERP销售订单号
 -- 效期字段标准化处理：支持多种格式（YYYY-MM, YYYYMM, YYYYMMDD, YYYY-MM-DD）
 CONVERT(NVARCHAR(50),
 CASE
 WHEN LEN(a.[效期]) = 7 AND ISDATE(a.[效期] + '-01') = 1 THEN
 DATEADD(DAY, -1, DATEADD(MONTH, 1, CONVERT(DATETIME2, a.[效期] + '-01')))
 WHEN LEN(a.[效期]) = 10 AND ISDATE(a.[效期]) = 1 THEN
 CONVERT(DATETIME2, a.[效期])
 WHEN LEN(a.[效期]) = 6 AND ISDATE(LEFT(a.[效期], 4) + '-' + RIGHT(a.[效期], 2) + '-01') = 1 THEN
 CONVERT(DATETIME2, LEFT(a.[效期], 4) + '-' + RIGHT(a.[效期], 2) + '-01')
 WHEN LEN(a.[效期]) = 8 AND ISDATE(LEFT(a.[效期], 4) + '-' + SUBSTRING(a.[效期], 5, 2) + '-' + RIGHT(a.[效期], 2)) = 1 THEN
 CONVERT(DATETIME2, LEFT(a.[效期], 4) + '-' + SUBSTRING(a.[效期], 5, 2) + '-' + RIGHT(a.[效期], 2))
 ELSE
 CONVERT(DATETIME2, '9999-12-31')
 END,
 23
 ) AS ValidityPeriod, -- 效期
 -- 生产日期字段标准化处理（逻辑同效期）
 CONVERT(NVARCHAR(50),
 CASE
 WHEN LEN(a.[生产日期]) = 7 AND ISDATE(a.[生产日期] + '-01') = 1 THEN
 DATEADD(DAY, -1, DATEADD(MONTH, 1, CONVERT(DATETIME2, a.[生产日期] + '-01')))
 WHEN LEN(a.[生产日期]) = 10 AND ISDATE(a.[生产日期]) = 1 THEN
 CONVERT(DATETIME2, a.[生产日期])
 WHEN LEN(a.[生产日期]) = 6 AND ISDATE(LEFT(a.[生产日期], 4) + '-' + RIGHT(a.[生产日期], 2) + '-01') = 1 THEN
 CONVERT(DATETIME2, LEFT(a.[生产日期], 4) + '-' + RIGHT(a.[生产日期], 2) + '-01')
 WHEN LEN(a.[生产日期]) = 8 AND ISDATE(LEFT(a.[生产日期], 4) + '-' + SUBSTRING(a.[生产日期], 5, 2) + '-' + RIGHT(a.[生产日期], 2)) = 1 THEN
 CONVERT(DATETIME2, LEFT(a.[生产日期], 4) + '-' + SUBSTRING(a.[生产日期], 5, 2) + '-' + RIGHT(a.[生产日期], 2))
 ELSE
 CONVERT(DATETIME2, '9999-12-31')
 END,
 23
 ) AS ManufactureDate, -- 生产日期
 IIF(a.[件装量] IS NULL, 1, a.[件装量]) AS PackingCapacity, -- 件装量
 b.StockType AS StockType, -- 库存类型
 a.[行号] AS RowIndex, -- 行号
 'ERP_YYY_GXXQ' AS WarehouseCode, -- 仓库编号
 -- ERP商品库存Id：货主ID_商品ID_批号_批次号_库存类型
 a.[货主ID] + '_' + a.[商品ID] + '_' + IIF(a.[批号] = '' OR a.[批号] IS NULL, '-', a.[批号]) + '_' + a.[批次号] + '_' + CONVERT(NVARCHAR(10), b.StockType) AS ERPGoodsStockId,
 -- 业务订单号：优先使用 [业务订单号]，为空则用 [业务单据号]
 IIF(a.[业务订单号] IS NULL OR a.[业务订单号] = '', a.[业务单据号], a.[业务订单号]) AS BusinessOrderNo -- 业务订单号
FROM
 [dbo].[ywlsb_center_v] AS a
 INNER JOIN [dbo].[ckhzdzb] AS b ON a.[仓库ID] = b.ckid AND a.[货主Id] = b.hzid
WHERE 1=1 and a.[商品ID]=@ERPGoodsId and a.[业务订单号]=@BusinessOrderNo

 ELSE
 CONVERT(DATETIME2, '9999-12-31')
 END,

 ELSE

 ) AS ValidityPeriod, -- 效期

 CASE

 CONVERT(DATETIME2, a.[生产日期])
 WHEN LEN(a.[生产日期]) = 6 AND ISDATE(LEFT(a.[生产日期], 4) + '-' + RIGHT(a.[生产日期], 2) + '-01') = 1 THEN
 CONVERT(DATETIME2, LEFT(a.[生产日期], 4) + '-' + RIGHT(a.[生产日期], 2) + '-01')
 WHEN LEN(a.[生产日期]) = 8 AND ISDATE(LEFT(a.[生产日期], 4) + '-' + SUBSTRING(a.[生产日期], 5, 2) + '-' + RIGHT(a.[生产日期], 2)) = 1 THEN
 CONVERT(DATETIME2, LEFT(a.[生产日期], 4) + '-' + SUBSTRING(a.[生产日期], 5, 2) + '-' + RIGHT(a.[生产日期], 2))
 ELSE
 CONVERT(DATETIME2, '9999-12-31')
 END,
 23
 ) AS ManufactureDate, -- 生产日期

 b.StockType AS StockType, -- 库存类型
 a.[行号] AS RowIndex, -- 行号
 'ERP_YYY_GXXQ' AS WarehouseCode, -- 仓库编号
 -- ERP商品库存Id：货主ID_商品ID_批号_批次号_库存类型
 a.[货主ID] + '_' + a.[商品ID] + '_' + IIF(a.[批号] = '' OR a.[批号] IS NULL, '-', a.[批号]) + '_' + a.[批次号] + '_' + CONVERT(NVARCHAR(10), b.StockType) AS ERPGoodsStockId,

 a.[业务类型] AS BillType, -- 业务单据类型
 a.[业务单据名称] AS BillTypeName, -- 业务单据名称
 a.[业务单据号] AS BillNo, -- 业务单据号
 a.[货主Id] AS ERPHZId, -- 货主Id（货主编号）
 a.[销售订单号] AS ERPOrderId, -- ERP销售订单号
 -- 效期字段标准化处理：支持多种格式（YYYY-MM, YYYYMM, YYYYMMDD, YYYY-MM-DD）
 CONVERT(NVARCHAR(50),
 CASE
 WHEN LEN(a.[效期]) = 7 AND ISDATE(a.[效期] + '-01') = 1 THEN
 DATEADD(DAY, -1, DATEADD(MONTH, 1, CONVERT(DATETIME2, a.[效期] + '-01')))
 WHEN LEN(a.[效期]) = 10 AND ISDATE(a.[效期]) = 1 THEN
 CONVERT(DATETIME2, a.[效期])
 WHEN LEN(a.[效期]) = 6 AND ISDATE(LEFT(a.[效期], 4) + '-' + RIGHT(a.[效期], 2) + '-01') = 1 THEN
 CONVERT(DATETIME2, LEFT(a.[效期], 4) + '-' + RIGHT(a.[效期], 2) + '-01')
 WHEN LEN(a.[效期]) = 8 AND ISDATE(LEFT(a.[效期], 4) + '-' + SUBSTRING(a.[效期], 5, 2) + '-' + RIGHT(a.[效期], 2)) = 1 THEN
 CONVERT(DATETIME2, LEFT(a.[效期], 4) + '-' + SUBSTRING(a.[效期], 5, 2) + '-' + RIGHT(a.[效期], 2))
 ELSE
 CONVERT(DATETIME2, '9999-12-31')
 END,
 23
 ) AS ValidityPeriod, -- 效期
 -- 生产日期字段标准化处理（逻辑同效期）
 CONVERT(NVARCHAR(50),
 CASE
 WHEN LEN(a.[生产日期]) = 7 AND ISDATE(a.[生产日期] + '-01') = 1 THEN
 DATEADD(DAY, -1, DATEADD(MONTH, 1, CONVERT(DATETIME2, a.[生产日期] + '-01')))
 WHEN LEN(a.[生产日期]) = 10 AND ISDATE(a.[生产日期]) = 1 THEN
 CONVERT(DATETIME2, a.[生产日期])
 WHEN LEN(a.[生产日期]) = 6 AND ISDATE(LEFT(a.[生产日期], 4) + '-' + RIGHT(a.[生产日期], 2) + '-01') = 1 THEN
 CONVERT(DATETIME2, LEFT(a.[生产日期], 4) + '-' + RIGHT(a.[生产日期], 2) + '-01')
 WHEN LEN(a.[生产日期]) = 8 AND ISDATE(LEFT(a.[生产日期], 4) + '-' + SUBSTRING(a.[生产日期], 5, 2) + '-' + RIGHT(a.[生产日期], 2)) = 1 THEN
 CONVERT(DATETIME2, LEFT(a.[生产日期], 4) + '-' + SUBSTRING(a.[生产日期], 5, 2) + '-' + RIGHT(a.[生产日期], 2))
 ELSE
 CONVERT(DATETIME2, '9999-12-31')
 END,
 23
 ) AS ManufactureDate, -- 生产日期
 IIF(a.[件装量] IS NULL, 1, a.[件装量]) AS PackingCapacity, -- 件装量
 b.StockType AS StockType, -- 库存类型
 a.[行号] AS RowIndex, -- 行号
 'ERP_YYY_GXXQ' AS WarehouseCode, -- 仓库编号
 -- ERP商品库存Id：货主ID_商品ID_批号_批次号_库存类型
 a.[货主ID] + '_' + a.[商品ID] + '_' + IIF(a.[批号] = '' OR a.[批号] IS NULL, '-', a.[批号]) + '_' + a.[批次号] + '_' + CONVERT(NVARCHAR(10), b.StockType) AS ERPGoodsStockId,
 -- 业务订单号：优先使用 [业务订单号]，为空则用 [业务单据号]
 IIF(a.[业务订单号] IS NULL OR a.[业务订单号] = '', a.[业务单据号], a.[业务订单号]) AS BusinessOrderNo -- 业务订单号

 [dbo].[ywlsb_center_v] AS a

 WHEN LEN(a.[效期]) = 6 AND ISDATE(LEFT(a.[效期], 4) + '-' + RIGHT(a.[效期], 2) + '-01') = 1 THEN
 CONVERT(DATETIME2, LEFT(a.[效期], 4) + '-' + RIGHT(a.[效期], 2) + '-01')
 WHEN LEN(a.[效期]) = 8 AND ISDATE(LEFT(a.[效期], 4) + '-' + SUBSTRING(a.[效期], 5, 2) + '-' + RIGHT(a.[效期], 2)) = 1 THEN
 CONVERT(DATETIME2, LEFT(a.[效期], 4) + '-' + SUBSTRING(a.[效期], 5, 2) + '-' + RIGHT(a.[效期], 2))
 ELSE
 CONVERT(DATETIME2, '9999-12-31')
 END,

 ) AS ValidityPeriod, -- 效期

 CONVERT(NVARCHAR(50),
 CASE

 DATEADD(DAY, -1, DATEADD(MONTH, 1, CONVERT(DATETIME2, a.[生产日期] + '-01')))

 -- 业务订单号：优先使用 [业务订单号]，为空则用 [业务单据号]
 IIF(a.[业务订单号] IS NULL OR a.[业务订单号] = '', a.[业务单据号], a.[业务订单号]) AS BusinessOrderNo -- 业务订单号
FROM
 [dbo].[ywlsb_center_v] AS a
 INNER JOIN [dbo].[ckhzdzb] AS b ON a.[仓库ID] = b.ckid AND a.[货主Id] = b.hzid
WHERE 1=1 and a.[商品ID]=@ERPGoodsId and a.[业务订单号]=@BusinessOrderNo

 -- 效期字段标准化处理：支持多种格式（YYYY-MM, YYYYMM, YYYYMMDD, YYYY-MM-DD）
 CONVERT(NVARCHAR(50),
 CASE
 WHEN LEN(a.[效期]) = 7 AND ISDATE(a.[效期] + '-01') = 1 THEN
 DATEADD(DAY, -1, DATEADD(MONTH, 1, CONVERT(DATETIME2, a.[效期] + '-01')))
 WHEN LEN(a.[效期]) = 10 AND ISDATE(a.[效期]) = 1 THEN
 CONVERT(DATETIME2, a.[效期])
 WHEN LEN(a.[效期]) = 6 AND ISDATE(LEFT(a.[效期], 4) + '-' + RIGHT(a.[效期], 2) + '-01') = 1 THEN
 CONVERT(DATETIME2, LEFT(a.[效期], 4) + '-' + RIGHT(a.[效期], 2) + '-01')
 WHEN LEN(a.[效期]) = 8 AND ISDATE(LEFT(a.[效期], 4) + '-' + SUBSTRING(a.[效期], 5, 2) + '-' + RIGHT(a.[效期], 2)) = 1 THEN
 CONVERT(DATETIME2, LEFT(a.[效期], 4) + '-' + SUBSTRING(a.[效期], 5, 2) + '-' + RIGHT(a.[效期], 2))
 ELSE
 CONVERT(DATETIME2, '9999-12-31')
 END,
 23
 ) AS ValidityPeriod, -- 效期
 -- 生产日期字段标准化处理（逻辑同效期）
 CONVERT(NVARCHAR(50),
 CASE
 WHEN LEN(a.[生产日期]) = 7 AND ISDATE(a.[生产日期] + '-01') = 1 THEN
 DATEADD(DAY, -1, DATEADD(MONTH, 1, CONVERT(DATETIME2, a.[生产日期] + '-01')))
 WHEN LEN(a.[生产日期]) = 10 AND ISDATE(a.[生产日期]) = 1 THEN
 CONVERT(DATETIME2, a.[生产日期])
 WHEN LEN(a.[生产日期]) = 6 AND ISDATE(LEFT(a.[生产日期], 4) + '-' + RIGHT(a.[生产日期], 2) + '-01') = 1 THEN
 CONVERT(DATETIME2, LEFT(a.[生产日期], 4) + '-' + RIGHT(a.[生产日期], 2) + '-01')
 WHEN LEN(a.[生产日期]) = 8 AND ISDATE(LEFT(a.[生产日期], 4) + '-' + SUBSTRING(a.[生产日期], 5, 2) + '-' + RIGHT(a.[生产日期], 2)) = 1 THEN
 CONVERT(DATETIME2, LEFT(a.[生产日期], 4) + '-' + SUBSTRING(a.[生产日期], 5, 2) + '-' + RIGHT(a.[生产日期], 2))
 ELSE
 CONVERT(DATETIME2, '9999-12-31')
 END,
 23
 ) AS ManufactureDate, -- 生产日期
